# The signals population of Digits_study01.
#
# One run per line: the run folder, the fold/seed directory, the scenario and
# the arm - the identity `signal_correlations.csv` carries in its `run` column.
# `#` starts a comment. 2471 runs.
#
#     foa signals --root "$FOA_STUDY_DIR" \
#         --population study/artifacts/Digits_study01/signals_population.txt
#
# WHY THIS FILE EXISTS. `foa signals` pools its correlations and its selection
# across every run it finds, so without a manifest the pass measures the disk
# it ran on rather than the study: a stage that lands afterwards re-weights
# every median and every share, silently. This pass was taken on 2026-09-01,
# BEFORE the blend's own screen (stage 20 of docs/REPRODUCE.md section 3) and
# the four joint-tuning extension stages (E1-E4) had run. It is the pass
# `tables/paper/signals_summary_extract.csv` and
# `tables/paper/signals_extras_extract.csv` are computed from, and therefore
# the pass `tab:signals` and the manuscript's `\nSignal*` macros print. Rerun
# over the tree as it stands today it pools 7,005 runs instead of these 2471,
# and eighteen macros move - which is a different measurement, not a
# reproduction of this one. So the population is frozen here, named rather than
# globbed, and the pass is repeatable from the published records.
#
# WHY NOT A PREFIX OR A STAGE LIST. The blend screen's 1,170 run folders and
# its ten finals are named `d01_reg*` and `d01_regfull*` - exactly like the
# regularisation screen and finals that ARE in this population. No prefix
# separates them. Only the folder names do.
#
# WHY IT IS NOT REOPENED WHEN A STAGE LANDS. The correlations answer whether a
# constraint-respecting signal tracks forgetting; adding a stage does not make
# the old answer wrong, it asks the question of a different population. A pass
# over the current tree is a legitimate thing to want and is one command away -
# drop `--population`. What is not legitimate is printing one population's
# numbers under the other's name.
#
# SIX OF THESE RUNS ARE NOT IN THE RECORDS RELEASE ASSET, and ship in the
# metadata core instead, under `study/artifacts/Digits_study01/`:
#
#   signalcheck_BaseTrainer_grid_search/...   the one-task smoke run that
#       preceded the stage and was never part of it. It carries a correlation
#       row, so the medians the shipped view prints are over it too.
#   d01_extreme_single_fold{1..5}/...         the five folds of the extreme
#       arrangement the stage no longer defines - the cohort's worst writer
#       alone. `report_tables.in_study` keeps them out of every table, every
#       stage total and every all-arms mean, which is also what keeps them out
#       of the asset `tools/build_records_asset.py` packs; they reach the
#       shipped views only through the run count.
#
# Both are records of runs that happened, and a pass cannot be reproduced
# without the runs it was taken over, so they are published rather than
# described. Only their `summary_*.json` and `accuracies_*.json` ship, as for
# every other run: no weights, here or anywhere.
